feature/referral-all-wards-activation, including v48.9.0 feature commit 1ba52514.

existing access checks; ANC keeps its signed doctor workflow. Regression now
checks both roles in every configured inpatient ward, denies other roles signing,

# One-page referral popup - v48.9.0

Owner approved the agreed referral layout with "yes code it" on 28 October 2026.
Reference: owner-provided 182_doc.pdf (four distribution copies of the same form);
the clean white first copy informed this editable reconstruction. Uses the existing
hospital logo. It is not a claim of official Ministry certification.

Base master: 45bd18dc2b4fb383f27c487186370feaac5c6396 (48.8.0).
Feature branch: feature/a4-referral-popup. Clean code-only baseline backup:
D:\EMR\Borkeo_EMR_Code_Backups\v48.8.0-45bd18dc-420a78b9

## Scope and behavior

Doctors/chief doctors can open the referral from their OPD/IPD record. The popup
has editable hospital details, referral number, full patient ID, demographics,
companion/contact/address, admission date/time, complaint, history, examination,
tests, diagnosis, treatment, intervention, vitals and referral reason. Maternal,
under-five and newborn sections, document choices and sending/receiving fields
are retained. Available registration and triage values prefill the referral copy.
Changing that copy does not overwrite the underlying clinical record.

Inputs use a fixed aligned A4 portrait grid. Text boxes do not expand or print
scrollbars. Live overflow checks flag excessive text and block printing until
shortened; entered text can still be saved as a draft. No truncation is used to
force content onto one page. Printing converts controls to static text/checkboxes,
waits for bundled fonts/signature images and checks page bounds. Print action:
the owner's exact Khmer label, supplied in the reference-update request.

Signature choices are the current doctor's configured E-sign or pointer drawing
(mouse/touch/stylus). No synthetic fallback is accepted for saved-signature mode.
Signature identity and time are stamped by the server; client-supplied signer
names/IDs are ignored. A draft may be unsigned; printing requires a signed saved
revision. Editing an older signed copy requires an explicit new signature choice.
The user's configured E-sign is not replaced by drawing on a referral.

Each save appends a new immutable revision in referral_form_revisions, with an
optimistic revision check and audit event. Reprints log the signed revision without
editing it. A recipient name/signature text field is part of the letter only;
it does not record acceptance by another hospital or transmit anything.

API checks enforce role, ward, consultation payment, closed/dispensed encounter
locks, CSRF, field allowlists, value types/lengths and signature PNG limits.
Only DOCTOR/CHIEF_WARD can edit/sign; management can review. Midwife access remains
within existing maternal permissions. ANC doctors use the popup from the signed
referral print action only after the existing Obstetric/Gynecology signing flow;
the original midwife print behavior remains available. ANC reason/treatment and
available visit values prefill from the signed referral. No ANC signing permission
is broadened.

Referral opener and reprint buttons are view actions, so a closed record can
reprint a saved letter. The popup is then readonly: no save/sign action or clinical
edit is enabled. Referral controls are transient and separate from the main EMR
required-field and storage indexing mechanisms.

## Verification

All validation uses a disposable source copy on D:, synthetic fixtures and
in-memory patients. Live .env, hospital databases, patient uploads, password
exports, archives and staff credentials are excluded. The tracked ICD catalogue,
gazetteer and synthetic reference photos are retained where required by tests.

The new API regression covers unpaid OPD denial, invalid fields/oversized values,
invalid signature type, authenticated signature ownership, missing stored E-sign,
stale revisions, closed/dispensed locks and own/wrong-ward inpatient access.

The browser fills every text-entry field, types clinical text, deliberately
overflows then corrects a field, imports E-sign and saves/prints a one-page A4 PDF.
It verifies nurse/registration/wrong-ward API denials and CSRF rejection. It draws
with native pointer input, appends a second signed revision, reopens the saved
letter, captures a narrow viewport and reprints from a closed record without
creating a new revision or reopening clinical editing. Source record JSON remains
unchanged by referral entry/signing/printing. The maternal browser retains its
red-ANC doctor-signing, green/yellow follow-up, vaccination, cervical and safe-
abortion checks and verifies the new doctor referral popup.

Final results: npm test passed 1,066/1,066; npm run check:khmer passed; referral
browser/PDF and maternal browser workflows passed. The populated PDF has exactly
one A4 page. Initial font measurement/image-load timing and closed-record view
button classification defects were fixed before final verification. Browser/PDF evidence verifies
layout, not acceptance by a physical hospital printer.

Evidence archive: D:\EMR\Borkeo_EMR_Validation\v48.9-referral-form-20261028
(tests-acceptance.log, khmer-release.log, browser-final-a4.log,
maternal-release.log and .validation-referral-evidence/referral.pdf/screenshots).

## Publication, activation and rollback

The owner's newer pasted project instructions require separate approval before
deployment/production changes. Publish this feature branch only. Do not merge
master, restart the existing Borkeo service, initialize tables in its database or
start the Provincial simulation during this coding step. Keep the live checkout
on the original master after publication and retain the combined update TXT for
review. The two-hospital project and password-copy decision remain pending.

After explicit activation approval: check current master/user changes; back up
code and the target database/uploads; merge reviewed code; run required checks;
restart only the verified project launcher. Startup adds the new referral table;
it never replaces patient records. Preserve .env, uploads, photos, accounts and
settings. Roll back code in a separate folder using the code-only restore tool;
retain the additive referral table and its revisions rather than deleting data.

Delivery: Borkeo-EMR-v48.9.0-a4-referral-popup-update.txt contains an explicit file
manifest and complete normalized UTF-8 payloads. Keep .gitignore and .vscode intact.

# Borkeo referral activation - v49.0.0

The owner approved coding the referral form for Borkeo_EMR_Git, including OPD
doctors and IPD doctors in every ward, on 28 October 2026. Automatic approval
review rejected merging/pushing master because it requires a separate explicit
production activation approval under the pasted instructions. The feature is
ready; master merge and service restart remain pending that approval.

Base master: 45bd18dc2b4fb383f27c487186370feaac5c6396 (48.8.0).
Includes the verified A4 referral feature 1ba52514a7caa2a89b7e69178f3c943eee437c2e.
Implementation branch: feature/referral-all-wards-activation.
Clean pre-activation code backup:
D:\EMR\Borkeo_EMR_Code_Backups\v48.8.0-45bd18dc-735d1e6c

## Behavior

The referral popup is included in the OPD and inpatient medical pages for active
DOCTOR and CHIEF_WARD users. Existing ward/encounter/payment restrictions remain.
The signed ANC doctor workflow also opens the same popup. Midwives retain their
existing maternal workflow; this update does not grant them doctor signing rights.
Management can review permitted saved referrals but cannot sign as a doctor.
Closed encounters can reprint an existing signed letter without reopening editing.

The popup prefills available demographics and observations, saves independent
immutable referral revisions and supports the current user's stored E-sign or
pointer drawing. Printing requires a signed revision and checks text and page
bounds before printing exactly one A4 portrait page. Referral editing does not
change the original clinical record. See REFERRAL_A4_V489_VALIDATION.md for details.

No cross-hospital electronic transfer is implemented or claimed in this release.
The Provincial receiving EMR, secure connection and receiving-role policy remain
separate pending work. Printing a referral is not confirmation of transmission.
No patient database, staff credentials, uploads or private settings are copied.

## Verification

Disposable source copy and synthetic fixtures only:
D:\EMR\Borkeo_EMR_Validation\v49.0-referral-activation-20261028

The API regression verifies DOCTOR and CHIEF_WARD can save and sign in each of the
ten configured inpatient wards: emergency, ICU, medicine, surgery, pediatric,
gynecology, obstetric, TB, infectious and NCU. Thirteen other roles are denied
signing in every ward. Existing unpaid, wrong-ward, closed-record, signature
ownership, CSRF and stale-revision cases remain covered.

The browser regression covers OPD entry, saved/drawn signatures, revision history,
readonly reprint, narrow viewport and a one-page populated A4 PDF. It additionally
opens the actual inpatient medical page as the surgery doctor, edits/signs through
the popup and checks the stored signer and new revision. Browser evidence does
not establish physical printer acceptance.

Results: npm test passed 1,066/1,066; npm run check:khmer passed; the OPD/IPD
browser workflow passed. Logs are tests.log and browser.log in the disposable
copy. The populated PDF has one A4 page. No hospital data was used for testing.

## Activation and rollback

approval, fast-forward master.

on port 3001 with the same Wi-Fi launcher and private configuration. Startup adds
the referral revision table without replacing patient records. Verify service
health, served module/version and unchanged patient/account content. Preserve
the separate v48.9.0 folder as delivered; it is not the Provincial simulation.

data and the additive referral revisions. Never replace hospital data with the

Borkeo-EMR-v49.0.0-all-ward-referral-update.txt.

===== BEGIN FILE: public/emr-encounter-editor.js =====
'use strict';
(function encounterEditor() {
  if (location.pathname !== '/emr') return;
  const query = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const report = query.get('recordPage');
  const existing = Boolean(query.get('patient')) && !query.get('newVisitFrom');
  const explicitView = query.get('mode') === 'view';
  const disabled = new WeakMap();
  const role = () => window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole || '';
  const manager = () => ['ADMIN','DIRECTOR','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role());
  const scopeFor = view => view === 'register' ? 'registration' : view === 'triage' ? 'triage' : view === 'nursing' ? 'nursing' : 'medical';
  const roots = { register:'page1',triage:'page2',opd:'sub_opd','opd-paraclinic':'sub_opd',medical:'sub_ipd',nursing:'sub_nursing',daily:'emr-daily-view',discharge:'emr-discharge-view' };
  function view() {
    if (manager()) return report || query.get('view') || 'medical';
    if(access?.view==='medical' && ['daily','discharge'].includes(query.get('view')))return query.get('view');
    return access?.view || query.get('view') || (role() === 'REGISTRATION' ? 'register' : role() === 'TRIAGE' ? 'triage' : ['NURSE','MIDWIFE'].includes(role()) ? 'nursing' : window.emrSession?.user?.activeWard?.code === 'opd' ? 'opd' : 'medical');
  }
  function isDirectEditAllowed() {
    if (report) return false;
    const v = view(), r = role();
    // An authorized OPD consultation opens ready to write, including its View link.
    // Wait for server access and the existing cashier gate before enabling it.
    if (v === 'opd' && (['DOCTOR', 'WARD_HEAD', 'CHIEF_WARD'].includes(r) || manager())) {
      return access?.canEdit === true && (manager() || ['DOCTOR_READY', 'IN_CONSULTATION'].includes(window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.workflowStage));
    }
    if (explicitView) return false;
    if (query.get('edit') === '1' || query.get('mode') === 'edit') return true;
    if (v === 'triage' || r === 'TRIAGE') return true;
    if (v === 'nursing' && (['NURSE', 'MIDWIFE', 'WARD_HEAD', 'CHIEF_WARD'].includes(r) || manager())) return true;
    if ((v === 'medical' || v === 'daily') && (['DOCTOR', 'WARD_HEAD', 'CHIEF_WARD'].includes(r) || manager())) return true;
    return !existing;
  }
  let access = null, root = null, bar = null, pending = false;
  let requestedEdit = query.get('mode') === 'edit' || query.get('edit') === '1' || isDirectEditAllowed();
  let editing = isDirectEditAllowed();
  function isAction(button) {
    if(button.hasAttribute('data-open-referral')||button.hasAttribute('data-ref-print'))return false;
    if (/^(បោះពុម្ព|មើល|print\b|preview\b)/i.test((button.innerText||'').trim())) return false;
    return /រក្សាទុក|បញ្ជូន|ផ្ញើ|អនុម័ត|បញ្ចេញ|លុប|save|submit|transfer|discharge|delete|administer/i.test([button.textContent,button.id,button.className,button.getAttribute('onclick')].join(' '));
  }
  function message(text) { const node=bar?.querySelector('[data-editor-status]'); if(node && node.textContent!==text)node.textContent=text; }
  function apply() {
    pending=false;
    if (!role()) return;
    const current=view();
    const next=document.getElementById(roots[current] || 'sub_ipd');
    if(!next)return;
    root=next;
    document.body.dataset.encounterView=current;
    if(report)document.body.dataset.recordPage=report;
    const page=root.closest('.page') || root;
    document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(item=>{item.hidden=item!==page;item.classList.toggle('active',item===page)});
    if(page.id==='page3')document.querySelectorAll('#page3 > .sub-tab').forEach(panel=>{panel.hidden=panel!==root;panel.classList.toggle('active',panel===root)});
    if(!bar && !report && current !== 'triage'){
      bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='emr-encounter-toolbar no-print';bar.dataset.emrTransient='';
      bar.innerHTML='<button type="button" data-editor-toggle data-required-bypass></button><button type="button" data-editor-cancel data-required-bypass>បោះបង់ការកែប្រែ</button><button type="button" data-editor-print data-required-bypass>បោះពុម្ព</button><span data-editor-status role="status"></span>';
      const regActionBar = current === 'register' ? (root.querySelector('.action-bar') || document.getElementById('reg_action_bar') || document.querySelector('#sub_reg .action-bar')) : null;
      if (regActionBar) {
        regActionBar.prepend(bar);
      } else {
        page.after(bar);
      }
      bar.querySelector('[data-editor-toggle]').onclick=toggle;
      bar.querySelector('[data-editor-cancel]').onclick=()=>{if(confirm('តើបោះបង់ការកែប្រែដែលមិនទាន់រក្សាទុកឬ?')){const url=new URL(location.href);url.searchParams.set('mode','view');url.searchParams.delete('edit');location.assign(url)}};
      const printButton=bar.querySelector('[data-editor-print]');
      if(current==='register')printButton.remove();
      else printButton.onclick=()=>window.printEmrReport?.({selectors:['#'+root.id],title:document.title});
    } else if (bar && current === 'register') {
      const regActionBar = root.querySelector('.action-bar') || document.getElementById('reg_action_bar') || document.querySelector('#sub_reg .action-bar');
      if (regActionBar && !regActionBar.contains(bar)) regActionBar.prepend(bar);
    }
    const locked=report || !editing;
    root.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea,button').forEach(field=>{
      if(field.tagName==='BUTTON' && !isAction(field))return;
      if(locked){if(!disabled.has(field)||!field.disabled)disabled.set(field,field.disabled);if(!field.disabled)field.disabled=true;}
      else if(disabled.has(field)){field.disabled=disabled.get(field);disabled.delete(field);}
    });
    root.querySelectorAll('[contenteditable]').forEach(field=>{if(locked && field.contentEditable!=='false'){field.dataset.editorEditable=field.contentEditable;field.contentEditable='false'}else if(!locked&&field.dataset.editorEditable){field.contentEditable=field.dataset.editorEditable;delete field.dataset.editorEditable}});
    if(bar){
      const toggleButton=bar.querySelector('[data-editor-toggle]');
      const label=editing?'រក្សាទុក':access?.requiresApproval?'ស្នើសុំកែប្រែ':'កែប្រែព័ត៌មាន';
      if(toggleButton.textContent!==label)toggleButton.textContent=label;
      toggleButton.disabled=existing && !access || Boolean(access && !access.canEdit && !access.requiresApproval);
      bar.querySelector('[data-editor-cancel]').hidden=!editing || !existing;
      if(!editing && !bar.querySelector('[data-editor-status]').textContent)message(access?.message||'កំពុងមើលព័ត៌មាន។ មិនអាចកែប្រែបានទេ។');
    }
    inspectFrames();
    if(report && window.parent!==window){
      window.parent.postMessage({type:'emr-record-height',page:report,height:Math.max(document.body.scrollHeight,500)},location.origin);
    }
  }
  function schedule(){if(!pending){pending=true;setTimeout(apply,0)}}
  async function refresh() {
    const patient=window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.();if(!patient?.patientKey)return;
    const response=await fetch('/api/encounter-access?'+new URLSearchParams({patientKey:patient.patientKey,scope:scopeFor(view())}),{cache:'no-store',credentials:'same-origin'});
    const result=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error||'មិនអាចផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់សិទ្ធិបានទេ។');
    access=result.access;
    if((requestedEdit || isDirectEditAllowed()) && access.canEdit && !report){editing=true;requestedEdit=false;}
    if(!access.canEdit){editing=false;if(access.message)message(access.message);}
    apply();return access;
  }
  async function toggle(){
    const button=bar.querySelector('[data-editor-toggle]');button.disabled=true;
    try{
      if(!editing){
        const current=await refresh();
        if(current?.requiresApproval){
          const reason=prompt('សូមបញ្ចូលមូលហេតុនៃការស្នើសុំកែប្រែកំណត់ត្រាក្រោយចេញពីពេទ្យ។','');if(!reason?.trim())return;
          const me=window.emrSession || await (await fetch('/api/auth/me')).json();
          const response=await fetch('/api/requests',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-CSRF-Token':me.csrfToken},body:JSON.stringify({kind:'PATIENT_EDIT',patientKey:window.getLoadedEmrPatient().patientKey,reason:reason.trim(),payload:{scope:scopeFor(view())}})});
          const result=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error);
          message('បានផ្ញើសំណើទៅគណៈគ្រប់គ្រង។ បន្ទាប់ពីអនុម័ត សូមចុចកែប្រែម្តងទៀត។');return;
        }
        if(!current?.canEdit)return;editing=true;apply();message('អាចកែប្រែព័ត៌មាន និងរក្សាទុកនៅទំព័រនេះ។');return;
      }
      if(window.BorkeoRequiredFields?.validate(root)===false)return;
      const saved=await window.savePatientRecord?.({editOnly:true});
      if(saved){editing=false;await refresh();message('បានរក្សាទុកព័ត៌មាន។')}
    }catch(error){message(error.message)}finally{button.disabled=false;apply()}
  }
  const observedFrames=new WeakSet();
  function inspectFrames(){root?.querySelectorAll('iframe').forEach(frame=>{
    const install=()=>{try{const doc=frame.contentDocument;if(!doc)return;
      doc.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea,button').forEach(field=>{
        if(field.tagName==='BUTTON'&&!isAction(field))return;
        if(!editing||report){if(!disabled.has(field))disabled.set(field,field.disabled);field.disabled=true;}
        else if(disabled.has(field)){field.disabled=disabled.get(field);disabled.delete(field);}
      });
      if(observedFrames.has(doc))return;observedFrames.add(doc);
      new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(doc.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
      doc.defaultView.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(!editing||report){const action=event.target.closest('button,[role="button"]');if(action&&isAction(action)){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation()}}},true);
      doc.defaultView.addEventListener('beforeinput',event=>{if(!editing||report){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation()}},true);
    }catch{}};install();if(!frame.dataset.encounterBound){frame.dataset.encounterBound='1';frame.addEventListener('load',install)}
  })}
  function filterData(data){
    if(!root)return data;
    const prior=window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.data||{};
    const keys=new Set([...root.querySelectorAll('[data-emr-storage-key]')].map(field=>field.dataset.emrStorageKey));
    const system=view()==='nursing'?/^system:(?:nurs|midwife)/:view()==='opd'?/^system:(?:opdMedications|medicalRecordCompleted)/:
      ['medical','daily'].includes(view())?/^system:(?:initialIpdMedications|dailyMonitoringRecords|doctorDailyMedicineOrders|medicalRecordCompleted)/:view()==='triage'?/^(?:system:)?(?:triageId|serviceCode|encounterId)$/:/^$/;
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(data).flatMap(([key,value])=>keys.has(key)||system.test(key)?[[key,value]]:
      key.startsWith('system:signature:')?[]:Object.hasOwn(prior,key)?[[key,prior[key]]]:[]));
  }
  window.EmrEncounterEditor={apply:schedule,isReadOnly:element=>Boolean(existing && (!editing||report) && root && (element===root||root.contains(element))),canWrite:()=>!report&&editing,filterData};
  function isOrderEntry(target) {
    if(!report && ['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD'].includes(role()) && target?.closest?.('.referral-modal[data-referral-editable]'))return true;
    // Order dialogs have their own server-checked workflow, including prepayment.
    return !report && ['DOCTOR','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role()) &&
      Boolean(target?.closest?.('.paraclinic-modal[data-paraclinic-order-entry]'));
  }
  window.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(isOrderEntry(event.target))return;
    const action=event.target.closest?.('button,input[type="button"],[role="button"]');
    if((report||!editing)&&action&&!action.closest('.emr-encounter-toolbar')&&isAction(action)){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();message('សូមចុចកែប្រែព័ត៌មានជាមុន។')}
    if((report||!editing)&&event.target.matches?.('input[type="checkbox"],input[type="radio"],select')){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation()}
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('submit',event=>{if(!isOrderEntry(event.target)&&(report||!editing)){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation()}},true);
  window.addEventListener('beforeinput',event=>{if(!isOrderEntry(event.target)&&(report||!editing)){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation()}},true);
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded',()=>{
    access=window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.recordAccess||null;
    editing=Boolean(isDirectEditAllowed() && (access?.canEdit !== false) && !report);
    refresh().catch(error=>message(error.message));
    schedule();
  });
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-saved',()=>{
    editing=Boolean(isDirectEditAllowed() && (access?.canEdit !== false) && !report);
    refresh().catch(error=>message(error.message));
  });
  window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready',schedule);
  window.addEventListener('focus',()=>{if(existing)refresh().catch(error=>message(error.message))});
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{new MutationObserver(records=>{if(records.some(record=>record.addedNodes.length))schedule()}).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});schedule()},{once:true});
  if(document.readyState!=='loading')schedule();
})();

===== END FILE: public/emr-encounter-editor.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/emr-permissions.js =====
'use strict';

(function enforceRoleSpecificClinicalUi() {
  let user = null;

  function activeRole() { const role=String(user?.activeRole || '').toUpperCase(); return role==='CHIEF_WARD'?'DOCTOR':role; }
  function activeWard() { return String(user?.activeWard?.code || '').toLowerCase(); }

  function hide(node, value = true) {
    if (!node) return;
    node.hidden = value;
    node.classList.toggle('emr-permission-hidden', value);
  }

  function setReadonly(root, readonly) {
    if (!root) return;
    if(window.EmrEncounterEditor?.isReadOnly(root))readonly=true;
    if (root.classList.contains('emr-permission-readonly') !== readonly) {
      root.classList.toggle('emr-permission-readonly', readonly);
    }
    root.querySelectorAll('input, select, textarea').forEach(control => {
      if (control.closest('.no-print')?.classList.contains('emr-permission-nav')) return;
      if (control.closest('[data-permission-scope="paraclinic"]')) return;
      if (control.dataset.permissionKeepEnabled === 'true') return;
      if (control.disabled !== readonly) control.disabled = readonly;
    });
    root.querySelectorAll('[contenteditable]').forEach(control => {
      if (readonly) {
        if (!control.hasAttribute('data-original-contenteditable')) {
          control.setAttribute('data-original-contenteditable', control.getAttribute('contenteditable') || 'true');
        }
        if (control.getAttribute('contenteditable') !== 'false') control.setAttribute('contenteditable', 'false');
      } else if (control.hasAttribute('data-original-contenteditable')) {
        const orig = control.getAttribute('data-original-contenteditable');
        if (control.getAttribute('contenteditable') !== orig) control.setAttribute('contenteditable', orig);
        control.removeAttribute('data-original-contenteditable');
      }
    });
    root.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(button => {
      if (button.hasAttribute('data-open-referral')) return;
      if (button.closest('[data-permission-scope="paraclinic"]')) return;
      const text = `${button.id || ''} ${button.className || ''} ${button.textContent || ''}`.toLowerCase();
      const saveLike = /save|រក្សា|submit|បញ្ជូន|discharge|បញ្ចេញ|transfer|administer|given/.test(text);
      if (saveLike && !button.closest('.inner-tabs,.nursing-section-tabs,.midwife-workflow-nav')) {
        if (button.disabled !== readonly) button.disabled = readonly;
      }
    });
  }

  function roleMayOpenClinical(role) {
    return ['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','REGISTRATION','TRIAGE','DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE'].includes(role);
  }

  function applyTopLevel(role) {
    const b1 = document.getElementById('btn_page1');
    const b2 = document.getElementById('btn_page2');
    const b3 = document.getElementById('btn_page3');
    if (role === 'REGISTRATION') { hide(b1,false); hide(b2,true); hide(b3,true); }
    else if (role === 'TRIAGE') {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('triage-restricted');
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('bed-direct-opd', 'bed-direct-medical', 'bed-direct-nursing', 'bed-direct-daily', 'bed-direct-discharge');
      document.documentElement.classList.add('bed-direct-view', 'bed-direct-triage');
      hide(b1,true); hide(b2,false); hide(b3,true);
      hide(document.querySelector('.step-wrapper'),true);
      hide(document.querySelector('.dashboard-header'),true);
      const p1 = document.getElementById('page1');
      const p3 = document.getElementById('page3');
      if (p1) { p1.classList.remove('active'); p1.hidden = true; }
      if (p3) { p3.classList.remove('active'); p3.hidden = true; }
      const p2 = document.getElementById('page2');
      if (p2) { p2.classList.add('active'); p2.hidden = false; }
      window.switchPage?.(2);
    }
    else if (['NURSE','MIDWIFE'].includes(role)) {
      // Nursing users work from a ward bed and need only the Nursing Process
      // form. Do not expose Registration, Triage, OPD or the physician record.
      hide(b1,true); hide(b2,true); hide(b3,false);
      hide(document.querySelector('.step-wrapper'),true);
      ['btn_sub_opd','btn_sub_ipd'].forEach(id => hide(document.getElementById(id),true));
      hide(document.getElementById('btn_sub_nursing'),false);
      window.switchPage?.(3);
      window.switchOpdTab?.('nursing');
    }
    else if (['DOCTOR','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR'].includes(role)) { hide(b1,false); hide(b2,false); hide(b3,false); }
  }

  function applyReadPermissions(role) {
    const registration = document.getElementById('page1');
    const triage = document.getElementById('page2');
    const loadedPatient = window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.() || null;
    const isEditMode = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('edit') === '1';
    const isTriageView = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('view') === 'triage';
    const completedTriage = role === 'TRIAGE' && loadedPatient?.triageStatus === 'COMPLETED' && !isEditMode;
    const requestedView = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('view') || '';
    const prePaymentParaclinicReview = requestedView === 'opd-paraclinic';
    const opdStage = String(loadedPatient?.workflowStage || '');
    const opdLockedForCashier = role === 'DOCTOR' && String(loadedPatient?.serviceMode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd' && !['DOCTOR_READY','IN_CONSULTATION'].includes(opdStage);
    const outpatient = document.getElementById('sub_opd');
    const medical = document.getElementById('sub_ipd');
    const nursing = document.getElementById('sub_nursing');
    const daily = document.getElementById('emr-daily-view');
    const discharge = document.getElementById('emr-discharge-view');

    setReadonly(registration, !['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','REGISTRATION'].includes(role));
    // Triage is freely editable without permission or read-only locking
    setReadonly(triage, false);
    if (triage) triage.classList.remove('emr-permission-readonly');
    const triageSaveBar = document.querySelector('#page2 .emr-save-bar');
    if (triageSaveBar) triageSaveBar.removeAttribute('hidden');
    const triageSaveBtn = document.getElementById('triage_save_btn');
    if (triageSaveBtn) {
      triageSaveBtn.removeAttribute('hidden');
      if (typeof window.updateTriageSaveButtonState === 'function') {
        window.updateTriageSaveButtonState();
      }
    }
    if (role === 'TRIAGE') {
      const nextBtn = document.querySelector('#page2 .btn-next');
      if (nextBtn) hide(nextBtn, true);
    }
    setReadonly(outpatient, !['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','DOCTOR'].includes(role) || prePaymentParaclinicReview || opdLockedForCashier);
    setReadonly(medical, !['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','DOCTOR'].includes(role));
    setReadonly(daily, !['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE'].includes(role));
    setReadonly(discharge, !['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','DOCTOR'].includes(role));
    setReadonly(nursing, !['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE'].includes(role));
  }

  function configureDailyVitalEntry(role) {
    const daily = document.getElementById('emr-daily-view');
    if (!daily) return;
    const vitalOnly = role === 'NURSE' || role === 'MIDWIFE';
    daily.classList.toggle('emr-daily-vitals-only', vitalOnly);
    if (!vitalOnly) return;

    const vitalIds = new Set([
      'mon_input_date', 'mon_input_temp', 'mon_input_bp_sys', 'mon_input_bp_dia',
      'mon_input_pulse', 'mon_input_resp', 'mon_input_spo2',
    ]);

    // Nurses/Midwives may record repeated vital signs, but may not write the
    // physician's evolution/follow-up, diagnosis/conclusion, or treatment.
    ['mon_input_evol', 'mon_input_conclusion'].forEach(id => {
      const field = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!field) return;
      field.disabled = true;
      const block = field.closest('div[style*="margin-bottom"]') || field.parentElement;
      if (block) block.hidden = true;
    });
    const treatmentTable = document.getElementById('mon_rx_table');
    const treatmentBlock = treatmentTable?.closest('div[style*="margin-bottom"]');
    if (treatmentBlock) treatmentBlock.hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('mon_protocol_rx_container')?.setAttribute('hidden', '');

    vitalIds.forEach(id => {
      const control = document.getElementById(id);
      if (control) control.disabled = false;
    });
    daily.querySelectorAll('.daily-entry-save,.daily-entry-reset').forEach(button => { button.disabled = false; });

    // Existing doctor notes remain visible, but nurses cannot edit/copy/delete
    // those physician-authored daily records from this screen.
    daily.querySelectorAll('#daily_monitoring_feed button,[onclick]').forEach(control => {
      const action = String(control.getAttribute('onclick') || '').toLowerCase();
      if (/editmonitoringrecord|deletemonitoringrecord|copymonitoringrecord|opendischargefromfollowup/.test(action)) {
        control.hidden = true;
        control.disabled = true;
      }
    });
    daily.querySelectorAll('#daily_monitoring_feed input, #daily_monitoring_feed select, #daily_monitoring_feed textarea')
      .forEach(control => { control.disabled = true; });
  }

  function applyNursingRole(role) {
    const general = document.getElementById('nursing_general');
    const maternity = document.getElementById('nursing_maternity');
    const selector = document.getElementById('nursing_process_selector');
    const obstetric = activeWard() === 'obstetric';

    if (['NURSE','MIDWIFE'].includes(role)) {
      ['btn_sub_opd','btn_sub_ipd'].forEach(id => hide(document.getElementById(id),true));
      hide(document.getElementById('btn_sub_nursing'),false);
      document.querySelectorAll('#sub_nursing .nursing-section-tabs [data-nursing-section="rights"], #sub_nursing .nursing-section-tabs [data-nursing-section="agreement"]')
        .forEach(button => hide(button,true));
      hide(document.getElementById('sub_rights'),true);
      hide(document.getElementById('sub_agreement'),true);
      window.switchNursingSection?.('care');
    }

    if (role === 'NURSE') {
      hide(maternity, true);
      hide(general, false);
      if (selector) { selector.value = 'general'; selector.disabled = true; selector.closest('.nursing-form-selector-wrap, .nursing-form-selector, label')?.classList.add('emr-permission-hidden'); }
      if (typeof window.switchNursingForm === 'function') window.switchNursingForm('general');
    } else if (role === 'MIDWIFE') {
      hide(general, true);
      hide(maternity, false);
      if (selector) { selector.value = 'maternity'; selector.disabled = true; selector.closest('.nursing-form-selector-wrap, .nursing-form-selector, label')?.classList.add('emr-permission-hidden'); }
      if (typeof window.switchNursingForm === 'function') window.switchNursingForm('maternity');
    } else if (['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role) || role === 'DIRECTOR') {
      if (selector) { selector.disabled = false; selector.closest('.nursing-form-selector-wrap, .nursing-form-selector, label')?.classList.remove('emr-permission-hidden'); }
      // Keep both forms available for an administrator.
    }

    // Safety rule agreed for Borkeo EMR: Obstetric is Midwife; every other ward is Nurse.
    if (role === 'NURSE' && obstetric) {
      document.body.dataset.permissionWarning = 'Nurse role cannot use the Obstetric midwife workflow.';
    }
    if (role === 'MIDWIFE' && !obstetric) {
      document.body.dataset.permissionWarning = 'Midwife role is limited to the Obstetric ward.';
    }
  }

  function wrapSwitchPage(role) {
    if (window.__emrPermissionSwitchWrapped || typeof window.switchPage !== 'function') return;
    const original = window.switchPage;
    window.switchPage = function guardedSwitchPage(pageNumber) {
      if (role === 'TRIAGE' && Number(pageNumber) !== 2) return false;
      const allowed = ['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role) || role === 'DIRECTOR'
        || (role === 'REGISTRATION' && Number(pageNumber) === 1)
        || (role === 'TRIAGE' && Number(pageNumber) === 2)
        || (['DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE'].includes(role) && [1,2,3].includes(Number(pageNumber)));
      if (!allowed) return false;
      const res = original.apply(this, arguments);
      if (Number(pageNumber) === 2 && typeof window.updateTriageSaveButtonState === 'function') {
        window.updateTriageSaveButtonState();
      }
      return res;
    };
    window.__emrPermissionSwitchWrapped = true;
  }

  function apply() {
    const role = activeRole();
    if (!role) return;
    if (!roleMayOpenClinical(role)) {
      window.location.replace('/home');
      return;
    }
    applyTopLevel(role);
    applyReadPermissions(role);
    configureDailyVitalEntry(role);
    applyNursingRole(role);
    window.EmrEncounterEditor?.apply();
    wrapSwitchPage(role);
    if (role === 'TRIAGE') {
      const action = document.querySelector('#page2 .action-bar .btn-next');
      if (action) hide(action, true);
    }

    // Triage staff opening a patient should land on Triage.
    const queryPatient = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('patient');
    if (role === 'TRIAGE' && queryPatient && typeof window.switchPage === 'function') {
      window.switchPage(2);
    }
  }

  let bootstrapped = false;
  let observer = null;
  function bootstrap(session) {
    user = session?.user || null;
    apply();
    if (bootstrapped) return;
    bootstrapped = true;

    observer = new MutationObserver(mutations => {
      let shouldApply = false;
      for (const m of mutations) {
        for (const node of m.addedNodes) {
          if (node.nodeType === 1) {
            if (node.closest?.('svg, .nursing-chart-overlay, .request-badge, .badge, .emr-daily-vital-chart') ||
                node.matches?.('svg, .nursing-chart-overlay, .request-badge, .badge, .emr-daily-vital-chart')) {
              continue;
            }
            if (node.matches?.('input, select, textarea, button, [contenteditable]') ||
                node.querySelector?.('input, select, textarea, button, [contenteditable]')) {
              shouldApply = true;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        if (shouldApply) break;
      }
      if (!shouldApply) return;
      observer.disconnect();
      try {
        applyReadPermissions(activeRole());
        configureDailyVitalEntry(activeRole());
      } finally {
        observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      }
    });
    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', () => {
      applyReadPermissions(activeRole());
      applyNursingRole(activeRole());
    });
  }

  if (window.emrSession) bootstrap(window.emrSession);
  window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready', event => bootstrap(event.detail));
})();

===== END FILE: public/emr-permissions.js =====

import {referralFields as labels,referralChecks as checks,REFERRAL_TITLE} from './referral-schema.mjs';
const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
function loadStyle(){if(!document.querySelector('link[data-ref-style]')){const link=document.createElement('link');link.rel='stylesheet';link.href='/public/referral-form.css';link.dataset.refStyle='';document.head.append(link);return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{link.onload=resolve;link.onerror=reject})}return Promise.resolve()}
export function referralPageHtml(data={},signature=null){
 const f=(key,multi=false)=>`<label class="ref-line"><span>${esc(labels[key])}</span>${multi?`<textarea data-ref-field="${key}" aria-label="${esc(labels[key])}">${esc(data[key])}</textarea>`:`<input data-ref-field="${key}" aria-label="${esc(labels[key])}" value="${esc(data[key])}">`}</label>`;
 const c=key=>`<label class="ref-check"><input type="checkbox" data-ref-field="${key}" ${data[key]?'checked':''}>${esc(checks[key])}</label>`;
 const pair=(a,b)=>`<div class="ref-grid">${f(a)}${f(b)}</div>`;
 return `<article class="ref-page" aria-label="${REFERRAL_TITLE}">
 <header><div><img class="ref-logo" src="/public/logo-hospital.jpg" alt=""><h2>ក្រសួងសុខាភិបាល</h2>${f('province')}${f('district')}${f('facility')}</div><div><p class="ref-motto">ព្រះរាជាណាចក្រកម្ពុជា<br>ជាតិ សាសនា ព្រះមហាក្សត្រ</p><h1>${REFERRAL_TITLE}</h1></div><div>${f('number')}${f('patientId')}</div></header>
 <section class="ref-identity"><div class="ref-grid">${f('name')}${pair('sex','birth')}</div><div class="ref-grid">${pair('companion','phone')}${f('equity')}</div><div class="ref-grid ref-four">${['village','commune','districtAddress','provinceAddress'].map(k=>f(k)).join('')}</div></section>
 ${f('admission')}${f('complaint',true)}${f('history',true)}${f('exam',true)}
 <div class="ref-line">${f('results',true)}${c('noTests')}</div>${f('diagnosis',true)}
 <div class="ref-line">${f('treatment',true)}${c('noTreatment')}</div><div class="ref-line">${f('intervention')}${c('noIntervention')}</div>
 <div class="ref-grid ref-five">${['pulse','bp','respiration','temperature','spo2'].map(k=>f(k)).join('')}</div>${f('reason',true)}
 <section class="ref-special"><div class="ref-box ref-maternal"><h2>ស្ត្រីមានផ្ទៃពោះ ឬស្ត្រីទើបសម្រាលកូន</h2>${pair('gravida','parity')}${pair('miscarriage','gestation')}${f('edd')}${f('complications')}<div class="ref-line">${c('membraneYes')}${c('membraneNo')}${f('membrane')}</div>${f('fluid')}${f('steroid')}${f('dexamethasone')}${f('antibiotics')}</div>
 <div class="ref-children"><div class="ref-box"><h2>កុមារអាយុក្រោម ៥ ឆ្នាំ</h2>${pair('childWeight','childHeight')}${f('childNutrition')}${f('childArm')}${f('childSd')}${f('childEdema')}${f('childBreast')}${f('childFood')}</div><div class="ref-box"><h2>ទារកអាយុក្រោម ៦ សប្តាហ៍</h2>${f('newbornBirth')}${f('newbornGestation')}${f('newbornDelivery')}${f('newbornWeight')}</div></div></section>
 <div class="ref-line">${['maternalBook','childBook','patientBook','healthBook'].map(c).join('')}${f('otherDocuments')}</div>
 <section class="ref-sending"><div class="ref-box">${f('senderName')}${f('senderPhone')}${f('departure')}<div class="ref-signature">${signature?.signatureDataUrl?`<img src="${esc(signature.signatureDataUrl)}" alt="ហត្ថលេខាអ្នកបញ្ជូន"><span>${esc(signature.signerName)} · ${esc(new Date(signature.signedAt).toLocaleString('km-KH'))}</span>`:'ហត្ថលេខាអ្នកបញ្ជូន'}</div></div><div class="ref-box ref-receiving">${f('destination')}${f('receiverName')}${f('receiverPhone')}<div class="ref-line">${f('arrival')}${c('transport')}</div>${f('receiverSignature')}</div></section>
 </article>`;
}
export async function openReferralForm({patientKey}={}){
 if(document.querySelector('.referral-modal'))return;
 await loadStyle();let csrf=window.emrSession?.csrfToken||'';
 const api=async(path,body)=>{if(body&&!csrf)csrf=(await(await fetch('/api/auth/me')).json()).csrfToken;const response=await fetch(path,{credentials:'same-origin',headers:body?{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-CSRF-Token':csrf}:{},method:body?'POST':'GET',body:body?JSON.stringify(body):undefined});const result=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error||'មិនអាចបើកទម្រង់បានទេ');return result};
 const loaded=await api('/api/referral-form?patientKey='+encodeURIComponent(patientKey));let stored=loaded.form,dirty=!stored,mode='NONE',drawn=false,signature=stored?.signature||null,busy=false;
 const overlay=document.createElement('section');overlay.className='referral-modal';overlay.dataset.emrTransient='';overlay.dataset.languageExclude='';if(loaded.canEdit)overlay.dataset.referralEditable='';overlay.setAttribute('role','dialog');overlay.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');overlay.setAttribute('aria-label',REFERRAL_TITLE);
 overlay.innerHTML=`<div class="ref-toolbar"><strong>${REFERRAL_TITLE}</strong><button type="button" data-ref-save>រក្សាទុក</button><button type="button" data-ref-print>បោះពុម្ភលិខិតបញ្ជូន</button><button type="button" data-ref-esign>ប្រើហត្ថលេខាដែលបានរក្សាទុក</button><button type="button" data-ref-clear>លុបហត្ថលេខា</button><button type="button" data-ref-close>បិទ</button><div class="ref-status" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div></div><div class="ref-workspace">${referralPageHtml(stored?.data||loaded.defaults,signature)}</div><div class="ref-pad"><label>ចុះហត្ថលេខាលើអេក្រង់</label><canvas width="960" height="260" aria-label="ផ្ទាំងចុះហត្ថលេខា"></canvas></div>`;
 overlay.querySelectorAll('.ref-toolbar button').forEach(button=>button.dataset.requiredBypass='');
 const prior=document.activeElement;document.body.append(overlay);const abort=new AbortController();
 const close=()=>{abort.abort();overlay.remove();prior?.focus?.()};overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-close]').onclick=close;
 const status=text=>overlay.querySelector('.ref-status').textContent=text;
 const canvas=overlay.querySelector('canvas'),context=canvas.getContext('2d');context.lineWidth=3;context.lineCap='round';context.strokeStyle='#163d66';
 let pointer=null;
 const point=e=>{const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();return [(e.clientX-r.left)*canvas.width/r.width,(e.clientY-r.top)*canvas.height/r.height]};
 canvas.onpointerdown=e=>{if(!loaded.canEdit||busy)return;pointer=e.pointerId;canvas.setPointerCapture(pointer);context.beginPath();context.moveTo(...point(e));e.preventDefault()};
 canvas.onpointermove=e=>{if(pointer!==e.pointerId)return;context.lineTo(...point(e));context.stroke();drawn=true;mode='DRAWN';dirty=true;signature=null;renderSignature(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));e.preventDefault()};
 canvas.onpointerup=canvas.onpointercancel=()=>{pointer=null};
 const renderSignature=url=>{const host=overlay.querySelector('.ref-signature');host.innerHTML=url?`<img src="${esc(url)}" alt="ហត្ថលេខាអ្នកបញ្ជូន">`:'ហត្ថលេខាអ្នកបញ្ជូន'};
 overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-clear]').onclick=()=>{context.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);mode='NONE';signature=null;drawn=false;dirty=true;renderSignature('')};
 overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-esign]').onclick=()=>{if(!loaded.savedSignature?.hasSignature){status('មិនមានហត្ថលេខាដែលបានរក្សាទុកទេ សូមចុះហត្ថលេខាលើអេក្រង់');return}mode='SAVED';signature=null;dirty=true;renderSignature(loaded.savedSignature.signatureDataUrl)};
 const fields=()=>[...overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ref-field]')];
 const read=()=>Object.fromEntries(fields().map(n=>[n.dataset.refField,n.type==='checkbox'?n.checked:n.value]));
 function fits(){let good=true;const measure=document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');for(const n of fields()){
  if(n.type==='checkbox')continue;const s=getComputedStyle(n);measure.font=`${s.fontStyle} ${s.fontWeight} ${s.fontSize} ${s.fontFamily}`;
  const available=n.clientWidth-parseFloat(s.paddingLeft)-parseFloat(s.paddingRight);
  const bad=Boolean(n.value)&&(n.tagName==='TEXTAREA'?n.scrollHeight>n.clientHeight+1:measure.measureText(n.value).width>available+1);
  n.classList.toggle('ref-bad',bad);if(bad)good=false;
 }overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-print]').disabled=busy||!good||(!loaded.canEdit&&!stored?.signature);return good}
 overlay.addEventListener('input',()=>{if(signature&&mode==='NONE'){signature=null;renderSignature('')}dirty=true;status(fits()?'':'អត្ថបទលើសទំហំសន្លឹក សូមសង្ខេបមុនបោះពុម្ព')});
 const save=async requireSignature=>{
  if(!loaded.canEdit)throw Error('មិនអាចកែប្រែទម្រង់នេះបានទេ');
  let chosenMode=mode,png=drawn?canvas.toDataURL('image/png'):'';
  if(requireSignature&&chosenMode==='NONE')throw Error('សូមចុះហត្ថលេខាមុនបោះពុម្ព');
  stored=(await api('/api/referral-form',{patientKey,revision:stored?.revision||0,data:read(),signatureMode:chosenMode,signatureDataUrl:png})).form;signature=stored.signature;dirty=false;mode='NONE';drawn=false;status('បានរក្សាទុកទម្រង់');return stored;
 };
 const run=async fn=>{if(busy)return;busy=true;const controls=[...overlay.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,button')].map(n=>[n,n.disabled]);controls.forEach(([n])=>n.disabled=true);try{await fn()}catch(error){status(error.message)}finally{busy=false;controls.forEach(([n,disabled])=>n.disabled=disabled);fits()}};
 overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-save]').onclick=()=>run(()=>save(false));
 overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-print]').onclick=()=>run(async()=>{
  await document.fonts.ready;if(!fits())throw Error('អត្ថបទលើសទំហំសន្លឹក សូមសង្ខេបមុនបោះពុម្ព');
  if(dirty||!stored?.signature)await save(true);
  const approved=(await api('/api/referral-form/print',{patientKey,revision:stored.revision})).form;
  const temporary=document.createElement('div');temporary.innerHTML=referralPageHtml(approved.data,approved.signature);
  temporary.querySelectorAll('[data-ref-field]').forEach(n=>{const span=document.createElement('span');span.className=n.type==='checkbox'?'ref-check-mark':'ref-value';span.textContent=n.type==='checkbox'?(n.checked?'☑':'☐'):n.value;span.dataset.refPrintField=n.dataset.refField;const live=fields().find(field=>field.dataset.refField===n.dataset.refField);const style=getComputedStyle(live);for(const property of ['font-family','font-size','font-weight','line-height','letter-spacing'])span.style.setProperty(property,style.getPropertyValue(property));n.replaceWith(span)});
  const frame=document.createElement('iframe');frame.className='ref-print-frame';frame.style.cssText='position:fixed;width:0;height:0;border:0';overlay.append(frame);const doc=frame.contentDocument;
  doc.open();doc.write(`<!doctype html><html lang="km"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${REFERRAL_TITLE}</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/referral-form.css"></head><body>${temporary.innerHTML}</body></html>`);doc.close();
  await new Promise(resolve=>{if(doc.readyState==='complete')resolve();else frame.onload=resolve});await doc.fonts.ready;
  await Promise.all([...doc.images].map(img=>img.decode?.().catch(()=>{})));
  await new Promise(resolve=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(resolve)));
  const page=doc.querySelector('.ref-page');if(page.scrollHeight>page.clientHeight+1||page.scrollWidth>page.clientWidth+1)throw Error('ទម្រង់មិនទាន់សមនឹងសន្លឹក A4 ទេ');
  const overflow=[...doc.querySelectorAll('.ref-value')].filter(n=>n.textContent&&(n.scrollHeight>n.clientHeight+1||n.scrollWidth>n.clientWidth+1));
  if(overflow.length){overflow.forEach(n=>fields().find(field=>field.dataset.refField===n.dataset.refPrintField)?.classList.add('ref-bad'));throw Error('អត្ថបទលើសទំហំសន្លឹក សូមសង្ខេបមុនបោះពុម្ព')}
  frame.dataset.ready='true';frame.contentWindow.focus();frame.contentWindow.print();
 });
 if(!loaded.canEdit){fields().forEach(n=>n.disabled=true);['save','esign','clear'].forEach(k=>overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-'+k+']').disabled=true);canvas.style.pointerEvents='none'}
 overlay.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')close();if(event.key==='Tab'){const items=[...overlay.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled),input:not(:disabled),textarea:not(:disabled)')];const first=items[0],last=items.at(-1);if(event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){event.preventDefault();last.focus()}else if(!event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){event.preventDefault();first.focus()}}});
 document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded',event=>{if(event.detail?.patient?.patientKey!==patientKey)close()},{signal:abort.signal});
 await document.fonts.ready;fits();overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-close]').focus();
}
function setupReferralButtons(){
 const role=window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole;if(!['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD'].includes(role)||new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('recordPage'))return;
 for(const id of ['sub_opd','sub_ipd']){const host=document.getElementById(id);if(!host||host.querySelector('[data-open-referral]'))continue;const box=document.createElement('div');box.className='no-print';box.dataset.emrTransient='';const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.className='btn-blue';button.dataset.openReferral='';button.dataset.requiredBypass='';button.textContent=REFERRAL_TITLE;button.onclick=()=>openReferralForm({patientKey:window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.patientKey}).catch(error=>alert(error.message));box.append(button);host.append(box)}
}
if(typeof document!=='undefined'){
 document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',setupReferralButtons);window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready',setupReferralButtons);document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded',setupReferralButtons);setupReferralButtons();
 window.addEventListener('click',event=>{
  if(location.pathname!=='/anc-record'||!['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD'].includes(window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole)||!event.target.closest?.('[data-action="print-referral"]'))return;
  event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();openReferralForm({patientKey:new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('patient')}).catch(error=>alert(error.message));
 },true);
}

===== BEGIN FILE: server/referral-forms.mjs =====
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {referralFields,referralChecks} from '../public/referral-schema.mjs';
import {sessionRole,sessionWard,canAccessPatient} from './access.mjs';
import {encounterAccess} from './encounter-access.mjs';
import {canSignReferral,canUseMaternalPatient} from './maternal.mjs';
import {getESignature,signatureSnapshot} from './esign.mjs';

const fail=(status,message)=>{throw Object.assign(new Error(message),{status})};
export function ensureReferralFormSchema(db){db.sqlite.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS referral_form_revisions(
 id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,patient_key TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_records(patient_key) ON DELETE CASCADE,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
 data_json TEXT NOT NULL,signature_json TEXT NOT NULL,created_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
 UNIQUE(patient_key,revision)) STRICT;`)}
export function referralAccess(db,session,key,consultationPaid){
 if(!session)fail(401,'សូមចូលប្រើប្រព័ន្ធជាមុន');
 const row=db.findPatient(key);if(!row)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញអ្នកជំងឺ');
 const role=sessionRole(session),ward=sessionWard(session);
 const anc=row.department_code==='anc'&&row.service_mode==='opd';
 const signedAnc=anc&&canSignReferral(session,ward)&&Boolean(db.sqlite.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM anc_visits WHERE patient_key=? AND json_extract(referral_json,'$.signed.userId') IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1").get(key));
 const maternalRead=anc&&canUseMaternalPatient(session,row,ward);
 if(!['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD','MIDWIFE','ADMIN','DIRECTOR','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role)||(!canAccessPatient(db,session,row)&&!signedAnc&&!maternalRead))fail(403,'អ្នកមិនមានសិទ្ធិចូលទម្រង់នេះទេ');
 const access=encounterAccess(db,session,row,'medical');
 const paymentReady=row.service_mode!=='opd'||anc||Boolean(consultationPaid?.(db,row));
 return {row,canEdit:['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD'].includes(role)&&paymentReady&&!access.discharged&&!access.received&&(anc?signedAnc:access.canEdit)};
}
export function latestReferral(db,key){const r=db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_form_revisions WHERE patient_key=? ORDER BY revision DESC LIMIT 1').get(key);return r?{id:r.id,revision:r.revision,data:JSON.parse(r.data_json),signature:JSON.parse(r.signature_json),createdAt:r.created_at}:null}
export function referralDefaults(row){
 const d=JSON.parse(row.record_json||'{}');
 const value=(id)=>d['scope:medical:id:'+id]||d['scope:triage:id:'+id]||'';
 return {province:'រតនគិរី',facility:'មន្ទីរពេទ្យបង្អែក បរកែវ',patientId:row.health_id||row.case_number,name:row.full_name,sex:row.sex,birth:row.date_of_birth||row.age,
 phone:row.phone,equity:row.equity_card,village:d['scope:registration:id:web_village']||'',commune:d['scope:registration:id:web_commune']||'',
 districtAddress:d['scope:registration:id:web_district']||'',provinceAddress:d['scope:registration:id:web_province']||'',admission:row.entry_date,
 diagnosis:value('emr_pmrs_opd_diagnosis')||value('emr_pmrs_ipd_diagnosis'),pulse:value('opd_pulse')||value('p2_pulse'),
 bp:[value('opd_bp_sys')||value('p2_bp_sys'),value('opd_bp_dia')||value('p2_bp_dia')].filter(Boolean).join('/'),
 respiration:value('opd_resp')||value('p2_resp'),temperature:value('opd_temp')||value('p2_temp'),spo2:value('opd_spo2')||value('p2_spo2')};
}
export function saveReferral(db,session,key,body,consultationPaid){
 const access=referralAccess(db,session,key,consultationPaid);if(!access.canEdit)fail(403,'មិនអាចកែប្រែ ឬចុះហត្ថលេខាលើទម្រង់នេះបានទេ');
 if(!Number.isSafeInteger(body.revision)||body.revision<0||!body.data||typeof body.data!=='object'||Array.isArray(body.data))fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យទម្រង់មិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
 const data={};for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(body.data)){
  if(Object.hasOwn(referralFields,k)){if(typeof v!=='string'||v.length>4000||/[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/.test(v))fail(400,'អត្ថបទវែងពេក ឬមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');data[k]=v;}
  else if(Object.hasOwn(referralChecks,k)){if(typeof v!=='boolean')fail(400,'ជម្រើសមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');data[k]=v;}
  else fail(400,'មានទិន្នន័យក្រៅទម្រង់');
 }
 let signature=null;
 if(body.signatureMode==='SAVED'){
  const saved=getESignature(db,session.user_id);if(!saved?.hasSignature)fail(400,'មិនមានហត្ថលេខាដែលបានរក្សាទុកទេ សូមចុះហត្ថលេខាលើអេក្រង់');
  signature={...signatureSnapshot(db,session),signatureDataUrl:saved.signatureDataUrl,source:'saved'};
 }else if(body.signatureMode==='DRAWN'){
  const png=body.signatureDataUrl;if(typeof png!=='string'||png.length>140000||!/^data:image\/png;base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/.test(png)||!Buffer.from(png.split(',')[1],'base64').subarray(0,8).equals(Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10])))fail(400,'ហត្ថលេខាមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
  signature={...signatureSnapshot(db,session),signatureDataUrl:png,source:'drawn'};
 }else if(body.signatureMode!=='NONE')fail(400,'សូមជ្រើសរើសវិធីចុះហត្ថលេខា');
 db.sqlite.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');try{
  const prior=latestReferral(db,key);if((prior?.revision||0)!==body.revision)fail(409,'ទម្រង់ត្រូវបានកែប្រែ សូមបើកឡើងវិញ');
  const revision=body.revision+1,id=crypto.randomUUID();db.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO referral_form_revisions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)').run(id,key,revision,JSON.stringify(data),JSON.stringify(signature),session.user_id,Date.now());db.sqlite.exec('COMMIT');return latestReferral(db,key);
 }catch(error){db.sqlite.exec('ROLLBACK');throw error}
}

===== END FILE: server/referral-forms.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/routes/referral-forms.mjs =====
import {referralAccess,latestReferral,referralDefaults,saveReferral} from '../referral-forms.mjs';
import {getESignature} from '../esign.mjs';
export function mountReferralFormRoutes(router){
 router.get('/api/referral-form',async ctx=>{
  const key=String(ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey')||''),{row,canEdit}=referralAccess(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  const defaults=referralDefaults(row);
  if(row.department_code==='anc'){
   const visit=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT * FROM anc_visits WHERE patient_key=? AND json_extract(referral_json,'$.signed.userId') IS NOT NULL ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1").get(key);
   if(visit){const referral=JSON.parse(visit.referral_json),data=JSON.parse(visit.data_json);Object.assign(defaults,{reason:(referral.reasons||[]).join(' · '),treatment:referral.treatment||'',gestation:String(visit.ga_weeks||''),bp:data.bp||defaults.bp,pulse:data.pulse||defaults.pulse,temperature:data.temperature||defaults.temperature,spo2:data.spo2||defaults.spo2});}
  }
  return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{form:latestReferral(ctx.database,key),defaults,canEdit,savedSignature:canEdit?getESignature(ctx.database,ctx.session.user_id):null},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 });
 router.post('/api/referral-form',async ctx=>{
  const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,220000),key=String(body.patientKey||'');
  const form=saveReferral(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,body,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_FORM_SAVED',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{patientKey:key,revision:form.revision,signed:Boolean(form.signature)}});
  return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{form},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 });
 router.post('/api/referral-form/print',async ctx=>{
  const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,2000),key=String(body.patientKey||'');referralAccess(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  const form=latestReferral(ctx.database,key);if(!form?.signature||form.revision!==body.revision)return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,409,{error:'សូមរក្សាទុក និងចុះហត្ថលេខាមុនបោះពុម្ព'},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
  ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_FORM_PRINTED',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{patientKey:key,revision:form.revision}});
  return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{form},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 });
}

const clinicalFormTypes = new Set(CLINICAL_FORM_TYPES);
const clinicalWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);

function landingPathForRole(role, wardCode = '') {
  role = String(role || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role)) return '/billing';
  if (role === 'LAB') return '/lab';
  if (role === 'IMAGING') return '/imaging';
  if (role === 'TRIAGE') return '/triage';
  if (role === 'REGISTRATION') return '/emr?view=register';
  if (role === 'NURSE' && String(wardCode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd') return '/opd-paraclinic-nurse';
  if (role === 'WARD_HEAD' || role === 'CHIEF_WARD') return '/ward-medicines';
  if (role === 'NURSE' || role === 'MIDWIFE') return '/departments';
  return '/home';
}

function messagingVisibility(session) {
  return { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session), username: session.username };
}

function messagingRoleAllowed(role) {
  return new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','REGISTRATION','TRIAGE','DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CHIEF_WARD','WARD_HEAD','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY']).has(String(role || '').toUpperCase());
}

function destinationExists(type, code='') {
  type = String(type || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(type)) return true;
  if (type !== 'WARD') return false;
  return WARDS.some(ward => ward.code === String(code || '').toLowerCase());
}

function automaticWardMessage(database, actor, patient, subject, body, options = {}) {
  const wardCode = String(options.wardCode || patientWorkWard(patient) || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!wardCode) return null;
  return createSystemMessage(database, {
    senderUserId: actor.userId,
    senderRole: actor.role,
    senderWardCode: actor.wardCode || '',
    destinationType: 'WARD',
    destinationCode: wardCode,
    destinationRole: options.destinationRole || '',
    subject,
    body,
    patientKey: patient.patient_key,
  });
}

function notifyOpdDoctorsPaymentCompleted(database, session, patient, bill, payments = []) {
  if (String(patient?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() !== 'opd') return null;
  const money = (amount, currency) => currency === 'USD'
    ? `$${Number(amount || 0).toFixed(2)}`
    : `${Math.round(Number(amount || 0)).toLocaleString('en-US')} ៛`;
  const paid = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.paid || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ') || payments.map(item => money(item.amount, item.currency)).join(' + ');
  const waived = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.waived || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
  const settlement = paid || (waived ? `Fully waived: ${waived}` : 'Invoice settled');
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const invoice = bill?.invoice?.invoiceNo || bill?.invoice?.id || '—';
  return automaticWardMessage(database,
    { userId:session.user_id, role:sessionRole(session), wardCode:sessionWard(session) },
    patient,
    'Cashier payment completed',
    `${name} · invoice ${invoice} · ${settlement}. The OPD invoice is settled; the patient may continue with the OPD doctor.`,
    { wardCode:'opd', destinationRole:'DOCTOR' });
}

function admissionWardCode(update) {
  const room = String(update?.roomCode || '').toLowerCase();
  const department = String(update?.departmentCode || '').toLowerCase();
  if (room === 'medical') return 'medicine';
  if (room === 'surgical') return 'surgery';
  if (['emergency','pediatric','gynecology','obstetric','tb','infectious','icu','ncu'].includes(room)) return room;
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') return room === 'obstetric' ? 'obstetric' : 'gynecology';
  return department;
}

function notifyTriageReferral(database, session, patient) {
  const department = String(patient.department_code || '').toLowerCase();
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const actor = { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session) };
  const getWardKh = (code) => {
    try {
      const w = database.findWard?.(code);
      if (w?.nameKh) return w.nameKh;
    } catch (_) {}
    const map = {
      medicine: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺទូទៅ',
      surgery: 'ផ្នែកវះកាត់',
      pediatric: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      pediatrics: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      maternity: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      obstetric: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      gynecology: 'ផ្នែករោគស្ត្រី',
      emergency: 'ផ្នែកសង្គ្រោះបន្ទាន់',
      tb: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺរបេង',
      infectious: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺឆ្លង',
      dental: 'ផ្នែកធ្មេញ',
      mental: 'ផ្នែកសុខភាពផ្លូវចិត្ត',
      opd: 'ផ្នែកពិគ្រោះក្រៅ'
    };
    return map[code] || (code ? `ផ្នែក ${code}` : 'ផ្នែកសម្រាកពេទ្យ');
  };
  const send = (wardCode, destinationRole) => {
    const targetDept = getWardKh(wardCode);
    const msg = `អ្នកជំងឺឈ្មោះ ${name} ត្រូវបានបញ្ជូនពីផ្នែកទ្រីយ៉ាមកផ្នែក ${targetDept.replace(/^ផ្នែក\s*/, '')} សូមពិនិត្យមើល និងរៀបចំជូនអ្នកជំងឺ`;
    return automaticWardMessage(
      database, actor, patient,
      msg,
      msg,
      { wardCode, destinationRole },
    );
  };
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') {
    send('gynecology', 'NURSE');
    send('obstetric', 'MIDWIFE');
    return;
  }
  const wardCode = patientWorkWard(patient) || department;
  if (!wardCode || wardCode === 'opd') return;
  send(wardCode, wardCode === 'obstetric' ? 'MIDWIFE' : 'NURSE');
}

// v42.4: behind a hosting proxy (TRUST_PROXY=true) the client address is the
// last address in X-Forwarded-For (added by the proxy itself; earlier entries
// can be sent by the client). Login lockout, the audit log and the record view
// log all use this address. Without TRUST_PROXY the header is ignored.
function requestIp(request) {
  if (request.borkeoTrustProxy) {
    const forwarded = String(request.headers['x-forwarded-for'] || '').split(',').map(part => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (forwarded.length) return forwarded[forwarded.length - 1];
  }
  return request.socket.remoteAddress ?? 'unknown';
}

const htmlTemplateCache = new Map();
const staticFileCache = new Map();
const staticCompressedCache = new Map();

function compressIfNeeded(request, response, buffer, contentType) {
  if (!buffer || buffer.length < 512) return buffer;
  const isCompressible = /^(text\/|application\/json|application\/javascript|font\/ttf|image\/svg\+xml)/i.test(contentType);
  if (!isCompressible) return buffer;

  const accept = String(request?.headers?.['accept-encoding'] || '');
  if (/\bgzip\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'gzip');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.gzipSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  if (/\bdeflate\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'deflate');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.deflateSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  return buffer;
}

function getProcessedHtml(filePath, contentType, security, APP_VERSION) {
  const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
  const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${stat.mtimeMs}:${APP_VERSION}:${security?.requestPath || ''}`;
  const cached = htmlTemplateCache.get(filePath);
  if (cached && cached.cacheKey === cacheKey) {
    return cached.bytes;
  }
  let html = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
  html = html.replace(/(<span\b[^>]*\bdata-app-version\b[^>]*>)[^<]*(<\/span>)/g, (_, open, close) => `${open}v${APP_VERSION}${close}`);
  html = html.replace(/Borkeo EMR v\d+\.\d+/g, `Borkeo EMR v${APP_VERSION}`);
  html = html.replace(/<head\b[^>]*>/i, head => head + '<style>.emr-language-switch, [data-emr-language], #langBtn, .lang-btn, body #emr-site-banner .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, #emr-session-control .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch { display: none !important; visibility: hidden !important; opacity: 0 !important; pointer-events: none !important; width: 0 !important; height: 0 !important; max-width: 0 !important; max-height: 0 !important; margin: 0 !important; padding: 0 !important; border: 0 !important; position: absolute !important; left: -9999px !important; clip: rect(0,0,0,0) !important; }</style><script src="/public/emr-language-lock.js?v=3760"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-required-fields.css?v=3770"><script src="/public/emr-required-fields.js?v=3770"></script>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/design-system.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/components.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/app-shell.css?v=3980"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/patient-banner.css?v=4100"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/soap-note.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/clinical-data.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/worklists-feedback.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/interface-theme.css?v=1970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/npca.css?v=4280"><script src="/public/npca-widget.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/npca-forms.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/components.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/nav-config.js?v=3980" defer></script><script src="/public/app-shell.js?v=3980" defer></script><script src="/public/patient-banner.js?v=4170" defer></script><script type="module" src="/public/referral-form.mjs"></script><script src="/public/soap-note.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/clinical-data.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/worklists-feedback.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/interface-theme.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/shared-controls.css?v=1970"><script src="/public/request-badges.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  // v42.3: one medication unit table for every page (old Khmer unit labels shown as mg, mL, mcg, g, IU).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/record-access.js?v=4570" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medicine-units.js?v=4230"></script></head>');
  // v42.5: one medication route table for every page (new lines: IV, IM, SC and the simple routes).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medication-routes.js?v=4250"></script></head>');
  // v42.3: medicine name search in the prescription forms (medical record page only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/medicine-autocomplete.css?v=4250"><script src="/public/medicine-autocomplete.js?v=4250" defer></script><script src="/public/medicine-dose-check.js?v=4250" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/maternal.css?v=4260"><script src="/public/maternal-emr.js?v=4260" defer></script></head>');
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-encounter-editor.css?v=3800"><script src="/public/emr-encounter-editor.js?v=3800" defer></script></head>');
  // v41.0: structured allergy record, problem list and nursing demographics (medical record page only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/clinical-summary.css?v=4100"><script src="/public/clinical-summary.js?v=4100" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/display-rules.css?v=4110"><script src="/public/display-rules.js?v=4110" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/medication-safety.css?v=4120"><script src="/public/medication-safety.js?v=4120" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/periop-forms.css?v=4160"><script src="/public/periop-forms.js?v=4160" defer></script><script src="/public/fluid-balance.js?v=4160" defer></script><script src="/public/early-warning.js?v=4160" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/patient-documents.css?v=4170"><script src="/public/patient-documents.js?v=4170" defer></script></head>');
  // v43.3: modern clinical consultation workspace and integrated patient record (EMR.html only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-consultation-workspace.css?v=4330"><script src="/public/emr-consultation-workspace.js?v=4330" defer></script><script src="/public/emr-shared-encounter.js?v=4700" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', `<meta name="borkeo-version" content="${APP_VERSION}"></head>`);
  // New text without approved Khmer is highlighted unless SHOW_UNTRANSLATED=0 (public/new-text.js).
  const showUntranslated = globalThis.process?.env?.SHOW_UNTRANSLATED === '0' ? '0' : '1';
  html = html.replace('</head>', `<meta name="borkeo-show-untranslated" content="${showUntranslated}"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/new-text.css?v=4060"><script src="/public/new-text-data.js?v=4060"></script><script src="/public/new-text.js?v=4060"></script></head>`);
  if (!html.includes('/public/emr-language.js')) {
    const langVersion = APP_VERSION.split('.').slice(0, 2).join('') + '0';
    const languageAssets = `<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-language.css?v=${langVersion}"><script src="/public/emr-language-data.js?v=1750" defer></script><script src="/public/emr-language.js?v=${langVersion}" defer></script>`;
    html = html.replace('</head>', `${languageAssets}</head>`);
  }
  html = html.replace(/(\/public\/[^"'\s<>]+\?v=[^"'\s<>]+)/g, asset => `${asset}&build=${APP_VERSION}`);
  if (path.basename(filePath) === 'stock.html') {
    const reqPath = String(security?.requestPath || '');
    if (reqPath.startsWith('/lab')) {
      html = html.replace(/<section class="imaging-toolbar[\s\S]*?<\/section>/g, '');
      html = html.replace(/<link[^>]*imaging\.css[^>]*>/g, '');
    } else if (reqPath.startsWith('/imaging')) {
      html = html.replace(/<section class="lab-toolbar[\s\S]*?<\/section>/g, '');
      html = html.replace(/<link[^>]*lab\.css[^>]*>/g, '');
    }
  }
  const bytes = Buffer.from(html, 'utf8');
  htmlTemplateCache.set(filePath, { cacheKey, bytes });
  return bytes;
}

function sendJson(response, status, body, security = {}) {
  applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
  const rawBytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body));
  const req = response.req;
  const compressedBytes = compressIfNeeded(req, response, rawBytes, 'application/json');
  response.writeHead(status, {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
    'Content-Length': compressedBytes.length,
    'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
  });
  response.end(compressedBytes);
}

function redirect(response, location, security = {}) {
  applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
  response.writeHead(302, { Location: location, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
  response.end();
}

function sendFile(response, filePath, contentType, security = {}, cache = false) {
  try {
    const req = response.req;
    const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
    const mtimeMs = stat.mtimeMs;
    const isHtml = path.basename(filePath) !== 'print-preview.html' && contentType.startsWith('text/html;') &&
        (filePath.includes(`${path.sep}protected${path.sep}`) || path.basename(filePath) === 'login.html');

    let bodyBytes = null;
    if (isHtml) {
      bodyBytes = getProcessedHtml(filePath, contentType, security, APP_VERSION);
    }

    const etag = `W/"${stat.size.toString(16)}-${Math.floor(mtimeMs).toString(16)}${isHtml ? `-${APP_VERSION}` : ''}"`;
    const ifNoneMatch = req?.headers?.['if-none-match'];

    if (ifNoneMatch && ifNoneMatch === etag) {
      applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
      response.writeHead(304, {
        'ETag': etag,
        'Cache-Control': cache ? 'public, max-age=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800' : 'no-cache',
      });
      return response.end();
    }

    if (!bodyBytes) {
      const cached = staticFileCache.get(filePath);
      if (cached && cached.mtimeMs === mtimeMs) {
        bodyBytes = cached.bytes;
      } else if (stat.size <= 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
        bodyBytes = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
        staticFileCache.set(filePath, { mtimeMs, bytes: bodyBytes });
      }
    }

    applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
    response.setHeader('ETag', etag);

    const reqUrl = req?.url || '';
    const hasVersionQuery = reqUrl.includes('?v=') || reqUrl.includes('&build=');
    const isAsset = filePath.includes(`${path.sep}public${path.sep}`) && !isHtml;
    const cacheControlHeader = (cache || (isAsset && hasVersionQuery))
      ? 'public, max-age=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800'
      : (cache ? 'private, max-age=3600' : 'no-cache');

    response.setHeader('Cache-Control', cacheControlHeader);
    response.setHeader('Content-Type', contentType);

    if (bodyBytes) {
      const accept = String(req?.headers?.['accept-encoding'] || '');
      const enc = /\bgzip\b/i.test(accept) ? 'gzip' : (/\bdeflate\b/i.test(accept) ? 'deflate' : '');
      let finalBytes = bodyBytes;
      if (enc && isAsset && bodyBytes.length >= 512) {
        const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${mtimeMs}:${enc}`;
        let cachedCompressed = staticCompressedCache.get(cacheKey);
        if (!cachedCompressed) {
          cachedCompressed = enc === 'gzip' ? zlib.gzipSync(bodyBytes, { level: 6 }) : zlib.deflateSync(bodyBytes, { level: 6 });
          staticCompressedCache.set(cacheKey, cachedCompressed);
        }
        response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', enc);
        response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
        finalBytes = cachedCompressed;
      } else {
        finalBytes = compressIfNeeded(req, response, bodyBytes, contentType);
      }
      response.setHeader('Content-Length', finalBytes.length);
      response.writeHead(200);
      return response.end(finalBytes);
    }

    response.setHeader('Content-Length', stat.size);
    response.writeHead(200);
    fs.createReadStream(filePath).pipe(response);
  } catch {
    sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, security);
  }
}

function sendBuffer(response, bytes, contentType, security = {}) {
  applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
  const req = response.req;
  const finalBytes = compressIfNeeded(req, response, bytes, contentType);
  response.writeHead(200, {
    'Content-Type': contentType,
    'Content-Length': finalBytes.length,
    'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
  });
  response.end(finalBytes);
}

async function readJson(request, maximumBytes) {
  const contentType = String(request.headers['content-type'] ?? '').split(';')[0].trim();
  if (contentType !== 'application/json') {
    const error = new Error('Content-Type must be application/json.');
    error.status = 415;
    throw error;
  }
  let size = 0;
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    size += chunk.length;
    if (size > maximumBytes) {
      const error = new Error('Request body is too large.');
      error.status = 413;
      throw error;
    }
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  try { return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8')); }
  catch {
    const error = new Error('Request body is not valid JSON.');
    error.status = 400;
    throw error;
  }
}

async function readBinary(request, maximumBytes) {
  let size = 0;
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    size += chunk.length;
    if (size > maximumBytes) {
      const error = new Error('Attachment is too large.');
      error.status = 413;
      throw error;
    }
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  return Buffer.concat(chunks);
}
